- **corund**: Red-on-revert as a Stop hook. When the agent says it is done, Corund reverts its non-test changes, re-runs the tests it just wrote, and refuses the stop if they still pass -- a test that passes without the code it is meant to cover did not test that code. Also denies git commit --no-verify and HUSKY=0. ([Grade-Inc/corund-plugin](https://github.com/Grade-Inc/corund-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/corund) · ★1 · 1 skill, 1 hook file)
